Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the sorting framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 650. The resulting operator achieves bound O(593/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_650(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 650. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^593(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The sorting operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 435. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 334 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the sorting decomposition. By minimality, the element a^435 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 334-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
